| `sync-blind-id` | done | `hiker-sync/src/crypto.rs::blind_id` (`HMAC(content_key, path)`); `hiker-sync/src/transport.rs` (all callers now derive blind_id from the doc's vault path: `buffer_update`, `drain_buffered`, `sync_via_server`, responder `DeltaRequest` reply) | path-keyed end-to-end: every blob the hub sees is keyed by `HMAC(content_key, path)`, derived deterministically on both ends from the doc's path so two devices reach the same stream without exchanging a logical id. Residual count/size/timing leakage → `sync-metadata-padding-deferred`. GC of an orphan stream on rename → `sync-rename-blob-rotation` |
| `sync-noise-channel` | done | `hiker-sync/src/transport.rs` (`noise::Config`) | Noise XX mutual auth from enrolled static keys (no PKI), forward secrecy, replay protection. **Cipher is ChaCha20-Poly1305** — libp2p-noise 0.56 doesn't expose AES-GCM for the channel; AES-256 lives in the content layer |
| `sync-content-encryption-aes256` | done | `hiker-sync/src/crypto.rs` (`ContentKey`, Aes256Gcm) | each file blob AES-256-GCM-encrypted client-side before it leaves the device; makes the server zero-knowledge. Scenario 6 |
| `sync-mdns-discovery` | done | `hiker-sync/src/transport.rs` (libp2p-mdns behaviour, continuous discovered-peer tracking + `start_discovery`) | continuous enrolled-peer discovery while `enabled && discovery` (feeds `sync-auto-sync`); manual "Discover (30s)" button remains. Tracking unit-tested; live-multicast e2e not run (sandbox) |
| `sync-auto-sync` | done | `app/src/bootstrap.rs` (auto-driver in the responder `select!`) + `sync_service.rs::{run_sync_round, auto_sync_round}` | always-converge while enabled: startup + ~15s interval + on-peer-discovered rounds; only deltas transferred; empty rounds silent; gated by `discovery` + the kill switch. Two-node auto-convergence test (no manual `sync_once`) in `sync_service.rs` |
| `sync-config-sanity-warning` | done | `app/src/sync_service/mod.rs::{config_unreachable_warning, SyncService::new}` (start-time `tracing::warn!` + `events_tx` line); `app/src/panels/sync.rs::config_warning` (page-rendered version off the snapshot) | one-time warning when `[sync].enabled` is true but the config can never reach a peer — peer mode with LAN discovery OFF and no `server_url`, so every round is a silent no-op. Fires at service start (so a headless / page-never-opened session still warns) AND the Sync page renders its own from the snapshot bits. Quiet when discovery is on, a server is configured, or sync is disabled. Test `config_unreachable_warning_fires_only_when_no_path_to_peer` |
| `sync-discovered-peers` | done | `hiker-sync/src/transport.rs` (single discovered map, read-time classification) + `app/src/panels/sync.rs` | discovered peers classified enrolled-target vs seen-unenrolled at READ time against the live shared enrolled set, so enrolling promotes to a sync target immediately (no mDNS re-announce wait); page shows "Discovered on LAN (enrolled)" + "Seen on LAN (not enrolled)" + a one-time log line per newly-seen un-enrolled peer. Tests `enrolling_a_seen_peer_promotes_it_to_candidate`, `enroll_promotes_already_seen_peer_for_next_round` |
| `sync-enroll-from-discovered` | done | `hiker-sync/src/crypto.rs::peer_id_to_fingerprint` + `app/src/panels/sync.rs` | each "Seen on LAN" row shows the fingerprint derived from the peer's Ed25519 identity-multihash `PeerId` + an Enroll button (verify-the-fingerprint caption; not blind trust); enrolling kicks an immediate round. Test `peer_id_to_fingerprint_round_trips` |
| `sync-config-section` | done | `core/src/config/sections.rs::SyncSection` + `mod.rs` registration | per-vault `[sync]` block: `enabled`/`mode`/`server_url`/`discovery`/`devices`/`device_name`/`device_names`; secrets excluded |
| `sync-ui-page` | done | `app/src/panels/sync.rs` + `tab.rs`/`toolbar.rs`/`tabs.rs` (`TabKind::Sync`) | Sync tab: state grid, copyable fingerprint, content-key copy/import, enrolled devices (alias + rename + remove), discovered-peer buckets + one-click Enroll (`sync-discovered-peers`/`sync-enroll-from-discovered`), Conflicts section (keep-mine/theirs/both), config-sanity warnings, manual peer-address connect, last-error line, in-page **Enable/Disable sync** buttons, Sync-now/Discover, recently-synced items + progress log; "Enable sync" button (not a dead-end hint) when off |
| `sync-settings-section` | done | `app/src/panels/settings/mod.rs::sync_section` + `core/src/config/patch.rs` (`sync.*` eligible, vault scope) | `enabled`/`mode`/`server_url`/`discovery` rows; `devices` read-only (enroll on the page); secrets excluded |
| `sync-dirty-buffers-toggle` | removed | — | **Removed 2026-06-03.** Was an opt-in to live-sync unsaved buffers by auto-committing dirty `working`→`accepted` on the autosave tick. Dropped in favor of a single model: **save = commit working = sync** (no auto-commit, no toggle, no disk-write/history pollution from unsaved edits). The autosave tick keeps only crash-recovery sidecars. A proper concurrent-editing mode (presence-scoped live `working` sync) is deferred — see `ideas.md`. The working-layer↔remote-edit reconciliation correctness fixes (localized `replace_working`, `three_way_merge` identical-twin skip, text-level working-mirror in `apply_remote_update`) were KEPT — they harden the peer-saves-while-you-edit path that exists in save=sync too |
| `sync-activity-provenance` | done | `app/src/panels/changes.rs` (`AuthorFilter::Sync`, `render_activity_row`) | synced ops render as their real op + `synced from <device>` provenance; "Synced" filter pill; `AuthorClass::Sync` already in `core/src/activity.rs` |
| `sync-disable-kill-switch` | done | `app/src/main.rs::reconcile_sync` + `sync_service.rs::shutdown` + `bootstrap.rs::Spawner::spawn_sync_service` (per-service cancel token) | off by default (nothing constructed); per-frame reconcile builds/spawns the engine when `[sync].enabled` flips true and tears it down when false — both live, no vault reopen |
| `sync-quic-transport-deferred` | planned | — | QUIC transport once UDP reachability is acceptable, or as an opportunistic upgrade with TCP fallback |
| `sync-metadata-padding-deferred` | planned | — | blob padding / cover traffic to blunt the server's count/size/timing metadata view |
| `sync-poke-on-commit` | done | `hiker-sync/src/protocol.rs` (`Message::SyncPoke`/`SyncPokeAck`); `hiker-sync/src/transport/mod.rs` (`poked: Mutex<bool>` + `take_poked`/`record_poked`, mirrors `take_newly_discovered`); `hiker-sync/src/transport/responder.rs` (`SyncPoke` dispatch → set flag, reply `SyncPokeAck`); `hiker-sync/src/transport/dialer.rs::poke` (connect → Hello/HelloAck → `SyncPoke`/`SyncPokeAck`, no manifest/delta); `app/src/sync_service/mod.rs` (`local_change: Arc<Notify>` + `notify_local_change` + debounced `spawn_poke_task`, 300ms coalesce, dials each discovered enrolled peer); `app/src/bootstrap.rs` (drains `take_poked` alongside `take_newly_discovered` in the responder `select!`, spawns the poke task); commit sites `app/src/editor_pane.rs::save_buffer` + `app/src/panels/canvas/render.rs::save_canvas_document` call `notify_local_change` after a successful `commit_working`; test `poke_sets_peer_poked_flag_once` (scenarios.rs) + `message_round_trips` covers wire serde | LAN baseline: a commit on this device pokes enrolled peers so they pull promptly instead of waiting up to ~15s for their own poll tick. The poke carries NO content — it only sets the peer's `poked` flag, which the bootstrap driver drains to fire an `auto_sync_round` (reuses the on-discovery trigger path). Debounced (300ms) to coalesce a save burst; non-blocking from the UI thread (`notify_one`); no-op with no peers / sync off. Role-gating (headless/editor flag) + keystroke-level streaming deferred. Transport round-trip unit-tested; the commit→notify→bootstrap-drain wiring is integration-level (verified manually) |


## Git transport (git.md)

The git transport behind the sync seam (`sync-transport-seam`): integrated (hiker drives commit + push/pull) and manual (the user drives git, hiker cooperates), both feeding the same 3-way merge + unified conflict surface. The `.md` is canonical; `.hiker/` is gitignored. All planned — no git transport is built yet.

| Slug | Status | Notes |
| ---- | ------ | ----- |
| `git-canonical-md` | partial | `app/src/git_sync/mod.rs::{manual_reconcile,fold_divergence}` — any working-tree divergence from the last-known commit folds via `OpLog::apply_external_edit` (the substrate's 3-way fold), so a commit-hook reformat / user amend is reconciled, not lost. GAP: the watcher-driven on-disk reconcile of a hook reformat (vs the git-round-driven one) shares the same `apply_external_edit` path but isn't git-engine-triggered |
| `git-ignores-hiker` | done | `hiker-git/src/repo.rs::Libgit2Backend::ensure_hiker_ignored` (idempotent `.hiker/` rule); called at engine construct (`git_sync/mod.rs::GitSyncEngine::new`). Test `hiker_dir_is_gitignored` (hiker-git/tests/repo.rs) proves a staged-everything commit excludes `.hiker/` content |
| `git-integrated-mode` | partial | `app/src/git_sync/mod.rs` (`GitSyncEngine` integrated mode): commit-on-save + push/pull driver (`bootstrap.rs::spawn_git_engine`). Empty remote = commit-only local versioning. GAP: `git gc` interval not yet run; inbound-merge surfacing through the in-editor conflict UI rides on `sync-unified-conflict-surface`'s deferred in-app pass |
| `git-commit-on-save` | done | `app/src/git_sync/mod.rs::{spawn_commit_task,commit_for_save_burst,commit_now}` — save pokes `notify_local_change` (`editor_pane.rs`), debounced (`commit_debounce_ms`) into one commit with the `Hiker-Author: user` trailer; `commit_now(_, amend=true)` `--amend`-coalesces. `hiker-git/src/repo.rs::commit_paths`. Tests `commit_on_save_produces_a_user_trailer_commit`, `amend_coalesces_into_one_commit`, `no_op_commit_returns_none` |
| `git-push-pull-rounds` | partial | `app/src/git_sync/mod.rs::push_pull_round` — pull then push on the startup + interval triggers (`bootstrap.rs::spawn_git_engine`); an inbound `Divergence` folds via `apply_external_edit` (`fold_divergence`), never `git merge`, so no git markers land in a file. `hiker-git/src/repo.rs::{pull,push}`. GAP: live push/pull over a real https/ssh remote is unit-tested only at the divergence-fold level (no network test); a rejected non-ff is logged + deferred to the next pull (never force-pushed) |
| `git-backend-trait` | done | `hiker-git` crate: `repo.rs::GitBackend` trait (open/init, gitignore, `commit_paths`, `commit_rename`, `pull`, `push`, `log`, `show`, `divergence_from`, `head_sha`) + `Libgit2Backend` impl. `git2`/libgit2 confined to this crate (vendored libgit2 + system OpenSSL); only plain-Rust types (`CommitInfo`/`Author`/`Trailers`/`Divergence`) cross the boundary. 12 tests in `hiker-git/tests/repo.rs` + `src/meta.rs` |
| `git-manual-mode` | done | `app/src/git_sync/mod.rs` manual mode: `push_pull_round` routes to `manual_reconcile` (never pulls/pushes); `commit_for_save_burst` reconciles a HEAD move then auto-commits only the user's still-uncommitted tree. Test `manual_mode_is_never_bidirectional` |
| `git-tolerate-head-move` | done | `app/src/git_sync/mod.rs::manual_reconcile` + `hiker-git/src/repo.rs::divergence_from` — detects HEAD moving / a dirty tree vs the last-known commit, folds each changed path as an external edit, then adopts the new HEAD as known (hash-gated + idempotent). Test `manual_head_move_folds_as_external_edit` (proves the user's out-of-band commit folds, no git markers) |
| `git-manual-commit-policy` | done | `app/src/git_sync/mod.rs::commit_for_save_burst` (manual branch) — reconcile-then-commit-only-if-uncommitted; `push_pull_round` short-circuits to `manual_reconcile` so manual mode never pushes/pulls/rebases/force-pushes |
| `git-observed-rename-commit` | partial | `hiker-git/src/repo.rs::commit_rename` + `app/src/git_sync/mod.rs::commit_observed_move` — a pure-rename commit (new path, byte-identical old content) carrying `Hiker-Rename: <from> -> <to>`, then an edit commit if content also changed. Tests `observed_rename_is_pure_rename_commit_that_follows` (`git log --follow`-matchable), `observed_move_is_a_pure_rename_commit_with_trailer`. GAP: `commit_observed_move` is not yet wired to the app's file-tree/dnd rename trigger — a rename currently rides the commit-on-save staged-everything commit (delete+add) without the dedicated rename commit |
| `git-attribution-trailer` | done | `hiker-git/src/meta.rs::{Author,Trailers}` — `Hiker-Author: <class>` mirrors the `.ops` frame's `Author` (user/agent:id/external/extractor:id/auto:id/sync:device), `Hiker-Rename: <from> -> <to>` on a move; render + parse round-trip, unknown author → external. Tests in `meta.rs` + `log` reads them back (`agent_author_trailer_round_trips_through_log`) |
| `git-parallel-history` | partial | `hiker-git/src/repo.rs::{log,show}` expose `git log` / `git show <sha>:<path>` for inspection; the authoritative history stays the `.ops` frames. GAP: not yet surfaced in the app's version dropdown / activity feed (those still read `.ops`, which is the intended cross-transport behavior) |
| `git-co-tenancy` | done | `.md` canonical (the fold reads disk, not HEAD — `git_sync/mod.rs::fold_divergence`); hiker never force-pushes / rebases / rewrites history (`push` never forces, manual mode never pushes); user hooks run untouched (hiker shells out no git, drives libgit2 directly, suppresses nothing) |
| `git-config-section` | done | `core/src/config/git.rs::GitSection` (`mode`/`remote`/`auto_commit`/`commit_debounce_ms`/`gc_interval_days`) + `SyncTransport`; eligible-keys in `patch.rs`; settings rows in `app/src/panels/settings/mod.rs::git_section`; selected via `[sync].transport = "git"` |


## Autosave (autosave.md)

Crash-recovery sidecar snapshots of dirty buffers, NPP shape (one file per buffer, overwritten in place per tick), plus a tab-state snapshot the next vault open round-trips. Backend-owned (`core::autosave`); separate from the op log.

| Slug | Status | Notes |
| ---- | ------ | ----- |
| `autosave-backend-module` | done | `core/src/autosave.rs` (`Autosave` struct, `core::autosave` module entry), the host (`autosave_*` commands as 5–15 line wrappers, `VaultSession.autosave` field). Same module-discipline shape as `core::store` — all `.hiker/autosave/` filesystem touches confined here, plain Rust types crossing the boundary |
| `autosave-store-layout` | done | `core/src/autosave.rs` (`Autosave::open` creates `<vault>/.hiker/autosave/`, `IndexFile` with `version`/`entries`/`tab_state` fields, atomic `write_file_atomic` helper write-temp-then-rename for both per-buffer `<id>--<slug>.md` files and `index.json`) | 
| `autosave-one-per-buffer` | done | `core/src/autosave.rs` (`Autosave::write` looks up existing `autosave_id` for the path and reuses it — same on-disk filename overwritten in place; `write_overwrites_in_place` test confirms id stability across ticks). NPP shape — no per-tick versioning |
| `autosave-write-tick` | done | `app/src/workbench_host.rs` (autosave tick every 5s while any tab is dirty; self-suspends when nothing's dirty and re-arms on the next dirty transition on vault open; flush-all runs synchronously on window blur). Per-path dedupe via a last-written cache so a buffer at the same content doesn't re-push every tick |
| `autosave-recover-cmd` | done | `core/src/autosave.rs` (`Autosave::recover` walks `index.json`, computes the live on-disk hash for each entry, drops matches + orphan-sidecar pointers from the index file, returns only the real deltas as `RecoveredEntry`). Host wrapper `autosave_recover` translates to `RecoveredEntryDto` for the frontend; tests `recover_drops_matching_on_disk` / `recover_surfaces_when_disk_differs` / `recover_drops_orphan_sidecar_pointer` cover the three branches |
| `autosave-recovery-auto-restore` | done | `app/src/workbench_host.rs` (recovery-and-restore opens every entry returned by `autosave_recover()` as a sticky tab — for files still on disk, loads autosaved bytes into the editor so the buffer reads dirty against `loadedText`; for deleted files, recreates the file with the autosaved bytes first, then opens normally as a clean buffer; both branches `autosave_discard` after the open since the autosaved copy is now live in memory). No modal — the dirty-marker on each restored tab is the recovery affordance, composing with the existing close-dirty modal + `editor-diff-vs-disk-toggle` |
| `autosave-tab-state-store` | done | `core/src/autosave.rs` (`TabState` with `open_paths`/`active_path`/`preview_path`/`saved_at_ms`; `save_tab_state` overwrites the slot in the same `index.json` rewrite path), `app/src/main.rs` (`persist_tab_state`: snapshots open-tab order + active + preview). `open_paths`/`active_path`/`preview_path` all key on `Tab::persist_key`, so a non-note active tab (canvas/board/home/…) round-trips, not just plain notes |
| `autosave-tab-state-silent-restore` | done | `app/src/bootstrap.rs` (`restore_tab_state`: reopens each `open_paths` entry as a sticky tab, then activates the one matching `active_path`, then opens `preview_path` if set and not already open; the nav stack is seeded from the active tab's real file path). Active/preview match on `persist_key` so every persistable tab kind restores as active; missing paths drop silently |
| `autosave-readonly-skipped` | done | `app/src/workbench_host.rs` (autosavable-path check filters out anything whose tab isn't a file buffer; the active-buffer text reader also short-circuits on non-file modes). Trash / snapshot previews therefore never enter the tick loop in the first place |
| `autosave-rename-clear-old` | done | `app/src/workbench_host.rs` (the watcher file events renamed branch clears the old autosave path after updating the buffer path). Next tick writes against the new path naturally — no special-case write |
| `autosave-no-watcher-suppression` | done | `core/src/watcher.rs` (`is_ignored` rejects any `.hiker/` prefix, line ~272). Autosave writes land at `.hiker/autosave/...` so they never reach the watcher's debouncer; no `Watcher::suppress_path` dance needed in `Autosave::write`. Test `is_ignored("'.hiker/index.db'")` already covers the rule |
| `autosave-vault-swap-clears` | done | `core/src/autosave.rs` (`vault_swap_reset` wipes the on-disk dir contents; `vault_swap_reset_wipes_dir` test). Each new vault gets its own `Autosave` instance constructed in `open_vault_at_inner`; `applyOpenedVault` stops the prior tick before recovery, runs `runAutosaveRecoveryAndRestore` for the new vault, then `start`s the tick. `onCloseRequested` clears each handled path + pushes empty tab state before destroy |
| `autosave-backup-class` | done | matches `design.md`'s sync/backup framing without further code changes — `.hiker/autosave/<id>--*.md` is regenerable from running memory (lost only on clean shutdown or modal Discard), `index.json` is durable (carries tab-state, not reconstructible after clean shutdown). "Back up the whole `.hiker/`" rule already covers both correctly |


## MCP (mcp.md)

All planned. v3 milestone — in-process MCP server in `mcp-server/` crate, Streamable HTTP transport, rmcp-backed, read + write tool surface, agent rollback via the `.ops` history. Architectural decisions (in-process, decoupled crate, transport choice, localhost-trust auth, etc.) live in the spec; the slugs below are concrete implementable features.

| Slug | Status | Notes |
| ---- | ------ | ----- |
| `mcp-server-crate` | done | `mcp-server/` is now a library crate; `hiker_mcp::start(McpDeps) -> McpServerHandle` spawns an axum task wrapping `rmcp::StreamableHttpService`. the host (`open_vault_at_inner` → `start_mcp`) brings it up on vault open; the handle drops on session swap, cancelling the task and removing the discovery file. `mcp-server/tests/smoke.rs` exercises the full HTTP path |
| `mcp-config-section` | done | `core/src/config.rs` (`McpConfig`, `McpToolsConfig`, `McpAuditConfig`); strict-load validates the section. Defaults: enabled=true, host="127.0.0.1", port=0, discovery_file=`.hiker/mcp.json`, max_top_k=50, tools.writes_enabled=true, tools.allow_redacted_lookup=false, every per-tool `<name>_enabled=true`, audit.log_full_input=false |
| `mcp-bind-host-configurable` | done | `core/src/config.rs::McpConfig::host`; `mcp-server/src/lib.rs` builds the bind address from `host` + `port` (with IPv6 bracket handling). Default stays `127.0.0.1`. The settings UI row carries the LAN-exposure warning text in its `desc` so the consequence is visible at the choice site |
| `mcp-tool-toggles` | done | `core/src/config.rs::McpToolsConfig::tool_allowed`; `mcp-server/src/handler.rs::guard_tool` called at the top of every `*_inner`; chat-agent path also filters `agent_tool_defs_filtered(...)` so the model never sees disabled tools. Live-applied via `Arc<RwLock<McpToolsConfig>>` shared between `VaultSession.mcp_tools` and the handler — `set_setting` swaps the contents in place so the next dispatch sees the new gate without a vault restart |
| `mcp-settings-ui-section` | done | `app/src/panels/settings/mod.rs` `mcp` section — bool / string / port-int rows for every eligible key, plus a read-only display for `discovery_file`. Defaults to vault scope (matches `[tasks]`); user scope works via the per-section toggle |
| `mcp-port-discovery` | done | `mcp-server/src/discovery.rs` writes `<vault>/.hiker/mcp.json` on bind; `McpServerHandle::shutdown` (and `Drop`) remove it. OS-assigned ephemeral by default; honors `[mcp].port` for a fixed port. Smoke test asserts both write and removal |
| `mcp-dynamic-capabilities` | done | `mcp-server/src/handler.rs` (rmcp `tool_router` advertises the v3 surface) | the mechanism is in place — the rmcp router is the natural seam for conditional advertising. No `is_available()` predicate yet because no feature-gated tool has landed; future feature-gated tools own their own predicates and will plug into the same router |
| `mcp-error-model` | done | `mcp-server/src/handler.rs::translate_hiker_err` translates `HikerError` → JSON-RPC error codes. Hiker-specific: 1002 (`note_not_found`), 1003 (`drift`), 1004 (`disabled`), 1005 (`indexer_unavailable`). `1001 vault_not_open` not wired — the server only exists while a vault is open, so `vault_not_open` can't occur over the wire today; reserved for the future "MCP outlives session" mode |
| `mcp-lifecycle-aware` | partial | per-spec, the lifecycle fields (`hiker.archived` / `redacted` / `retired`) aren't yet implemented in hiker so the filter is a no-op. The redacted-body restriction lives on `core::store::get_note` rather than the MCP layer when it lands |
| `mcp-audit-log-mcp-calls` | done | `mcp-server/src/audit.rs::AuditLog` appends one JSONL row per call to `<vault>/.hiker/agent-log/<YYYY-MM-DD>.jsonl` with `surface="mcp-tool-call"`. When `[mcp.audit] log_full_input = false` (default), bulky fields (`content`, `query`, `fields`) are redacted to `{redacted: true, len: N}` |
| `mcp-tool-search-notes` | done | `mcp-server/src/handler.rs::HikerHandler::search_notes` wraps `core::search::query`; returns lexical_hits + semantic_hits + fused. `top_k` clamps the fused bucket to `[mcp] max_top_k`. Embedder unavailability surfaces as `1005 indexer_unavailable` |
| `mcp-tool-get-note` | done | `HikerHandler::get_note`; `detail = digest|snippet|full`. Snippet uses chunk 0 from the store when available, fallback head-of-file otherwise. Missing files return `1002 note_not_found` |
| `mcp-tool-related-notes` | done | `HikerHandler::related_notes` calls `Store::related_notes`; `top_k` capped by `[mcp] max_top_k`. Unindexed source returns an empty vec rather than erroring |
| `mcp-tool-get-active-note` | done | `mcp-server/src/ui_context.rs`, `mcp-server/src/handler/` (dispatch + router), `mcp-server/tests/smoke.rs` | returns the focused buffer tab's vault-rel path + cursor byte offset + (if non-empty) selection `{start_byte, end_byte}`. Returns `{path: null}` for app-page / non-buffer tabs. Read-only; per-tool toggle `[mcp.tools].get_active_note_enabled`. Does NOT populate the per-session read set (only `get_note` does, per `mcp-read-before-write`) |
| `mcp-tool-get-open-notes` | done | `mcp-server/src/ui_context.rs`, `mcp-server/src/handler/` (dispatch + router), `mcp-server/tests/smoke.rs` | returns the ordered list of open `buffer` tabs as `[{path, active}]`; non-buffer kinds omitted. Per-tool toggle `[mcp.tools].get_open_notes_enabled`. Read-only; does NOT populate the read set |
| `mcp-tool-get-selection` | done | `mcp-server/src/ui_context.rs`, `mcp-server/src/handler/` (dispatch + router), `mcp-server/tests/smoke.rs` | returns `{path, start_byte, end_byte, text}` for the active buffer's non-empty selection; `{path: null}` when empty or no buffer. Per-tool toggle `[mcp.tools].get_selection_enabled`. Read-only; does NOT populate the read set |
| `mcp-tool-write-note` | done | `core/src/ops/agent.rs::write_note` + `HikerHandler::write_note`. Watcher-suppresses, writes via `vault.write_file_checked` when `expected_hash` is set, queues a pending op-log op (`author='agent:<client_id>'`, whole-body `Replace`) via `op_writes::stage_agent_edits`, enqueues `IndexJob::Upsert` |
| `mcp-tool-edit-note` | partial | `mcp-server/src/handler/dispatch.rs::apply_edits` — span-anchored patch tool, advertised in `tool_router` + `agent_tool_defs_filtered`. Direct mode (review off) validates + applies all edits transactionally via `apply_edit` and routes through `agent_write_note` once. Review-on mode stages op-log pending edits via `core::ops::op_writes::stage_agent_edits` (one anchored `Replace` per edit, all sharing the returned `batch_id`, author `agent:<client-id>`), reviewed through the op-log pending surfaces. A `replace_all` edit with >1 match collapses the call to one anchorless whole-body op (the op-log anchor must resolve uniquely). **Partial**: rule 5 (read-before-write) is owned by `mcp-read-before-write` (still planned); the per-tool `edit_note_enabled` toggle row is wired in the settings UI |
| `mcp-edit-note-validation` | partial | `mcp-server/src/handler.rs::edit_note_inner` enforces rules 1–4: path exists (else `1002`), per-edit anchor unique unless `replace_all: true` (else `1003` for missing, `invalid_params` for non-unique), no overlap between resolved byte ranges across edits (`invalid_params` naming the offending pair), all anchors resolve against the pre-application file content (single read up front; never sequential dependency). Smoke tests cover each branch. **Partial**: rule 5 (per-session read set) lands with `mcp-read-before-write` |
| `mcp-author-stamp-on-create-only` | planned | drop the automatic `hiker.author: agent-authored` stamp on every agent write; stamp only when `write_note` creates a previously-non-existent path. `edit_note` / `set_frontmatter` / `apply_tag` never stamp. Updates `mcp-tool-write-note` / `mcp-tool-set-frontmatter` / `mcp-tool-apply-tag-remove-tag` to drop the stamp call |
| `mcp-read-before-write` | planned | per-session `ReadSet` in `mcp-server/src/handler.rs` populated by successful `get_note` calls; `edit_note` always requires a prior read, `write_note` requires one when target path exists on disk (creates exempt). Violations return `1008 read_required` before validation. Foot-gun guard against hallucinated-anchor edits |
| `mcp-tool-set-frontmatter` | done | new `core/src/frontmatter.rs` (split/merge/assemble); `core::ops::agent_set_frontmatter` reads existing, deep-merges patch via `merge_agent_patch`, stamps `hiker.author: agent-authored`, routes through `agent_write_note`. Errors `invalid_params` if `fields` isn't a JSON object |
| `mcp-ui-refresh-on-agent-write` | done | `app/src/workbench_host.rs` (agent-write handler) — listens on op-log append events, gates on `author.startsWith("agent:")`, and runs the same tree-refresh / vault-home / active-buffer-reload sequence the watcher handler does. Dirty buffer is kept (toast surfaces the conflict) rather than prompting modally — agent writes are server-driven so an interrupt prompt would surprise the user |
| `mcp-tool-apply-tag-remove-tag` | done | `core::ops::agent_apply_tag` / `agent_remove_tag` thin wrappers over `agent_set_frontmatter` operating on the `tags` list. Idempotent (no-op when tag is already present / absent) |
| `mcp-write-tools-staging-aware` | partial | `mcp-server/src/handler/dispatch.rs::save_note` / `merge_frontmatter` / `update_tag` stage an op-log pending edit (via `op_writes::stage_agent_edits`) when `[mcp.tools].review_required` is on and return `status: "staged"` + `proposal_id`. Tool description strings call out the staged behavior so agents don't read a staged write as a failed write. **Partial**: (a) the agent-facing introspection tools (`mcp-tool-list-pending-proposals`, `mcp-tool-get-pending-proposal`) are specced but not implemented; (b) `mcp-tool-edit-note` adds `propose_batch` and per-edit proposals per `staging-per-edit-proposals` |
| `mcp-staging-read-disk-only` | done | `HikerHandler::get_note` resolves `vault.abs_path(rel_path)` and returns `1002 note_not_found` when the file doesn't exist on disk; pending proposals are not transparently surfaced. Agents discover staged content via the introspection tools, keeping the disk-vs-staging boundary explicit |
| `mcp-tool-list-pending-proposals` | planned | wraps the op-log pending query (`op_writes::list_pending_proposals`) with default `surface = "mcp-tool-call"` so the agent sees only MCP-originated proposals. Returns id + target_path + action + surface + session_id + created_at + content_hash; no body. Read-only; honors per-tool toggle. Lets an agent confirm its staged write landed when `get_note` returns 1002 |
| `mcp-tool-get-pending-proposal` | planned | wraps the op-log pending lookup by id; returns full metadata + proposed body. `1002`-style error when id is unknown (accepted / rejected / never existed). Agents cannot accept or reject from MCP — that stays a human-in-the-loop action |
| `mcp-pending-proposal-anchor-status` | planned | extends `get_pending_proposal` response: for `edit_note`-shaped proposals, recompute anchor match state on the fly against buffer-if-open-else-disk and return an `anchors: [{ edit_index, anchor_status: "holds"\|"drifted"\|"ambiguous", old_str_preview }]` array plus `target: "buffer"\|"disk"`. Per-edit, joint-batch (resolved via shared `batch_id`); omitted entirely for whole-file shapes. Racy by construction (true at read time only). Lets the agent detect doomed staged edits in a concurrent-edit workflow and amend before the user clicks Accept, instead of finding out via drift-at-apply |
| `mcp-tool-amend-pending-proposal` | planned | new MCP write tool letting an agent revise its own pending proposal in place before the user reviews. Same-client only; whole-file shapes only (`write_note` / `set_frontmatter` / `apply_tag`); `edit_note` batch-shape amend deferred. No version history — overwrites the stored body, bumps `metadata.amended_at_ms` + `metadata.amend_count`, recomputes `content_hash`. Fires op-log change events so an open review surface re-renders. User-accept races resolve in the pending store's transaction (last-write to the amend). Per-tool toggle `[mcp.tools].amend_pending_proposal_enabled`. Unblocks the "agent realized its first attempt was wrong" workflow without breaking the one-human-gate-per-change model |
| `mcp-tool-trails-list` | planned | enumerate trails with optional filters (containing-note, recently-activated, name-substring); returns id + title + waypoint count + activation timestamp + path. Lands with `trails.md` |
| `mcp-tool-trail-get` | planned | fetch a trail's full body + ordered waypoint list (each waypoint's source ref + annotation body); detail levels `digest` / `full`. Lands with `trails.md` |
| `mcp-tool-trails-containing-note` | planned | reverse lookup; returns trails that include a given note as a waypoint. Lands with `trails.md` |
| `mcp-tool-trail-create` | planned | create a new trail; placement per `trails-default-location`; routes through `core::ops::agent_*` so writes appear in the activity feed with `author='agent:<client-id>'` and ride the op-log pending/patch-review path like any other agent write (drafts removed 2026-06-05) |
| `mcp-tool-trail-append-waypoint` | planned | append a waypoint to a trail; `parent_waypoint_path` arg makes the new waypoint a side-trail child of the given parent (omitted → root-level append); creates the waypoint-note in the trail-doc's visible companion folder (`trail-storage-layout`), links to source, seeds optional starter annotation (default empty body); same agent-write routing as `mcp-tool-trail-create` |
| `mcp-tool-trail-remove-waypoint` | planned | remove a waypoint from a trail; cascades to descendants when target has children (per `trails-mode-remove-waypoint-verb`'s cascade rule); routes waypoint-note delete through `core::ops::delete` |
| `mcp-tool-boards-list` | done | `mcp-server/src/handler/{router,dispatch}.rs` (`boards_list` → `enumerate_boards`) wraps `core::boards::list`; read-only; per-tool toggle `mcp.tools.boards_list_enabled` |
| `mcp-tool-board-get` | done | `mcp-server/src/handler/{router,dispatch}.rs` (`board_get` → `fetch_board`) wraps `core::boards::get_board` (body + resolved columns/cards); per-tool toggle `mcp.tools.board_get_enabled` |
| `mcp-tool-board-add-card` | done | `mcp-server/src/handler/dispatch/boards.rs::add_board_card`; idempotent per board (`status:"noop"`); review mode stages via `stage_whole_body` (`status:"staged"`), direct mode commits via `op_writes::user_save` (`status:"written"`); `core::boards::add_card_preview` reads the board-doc only (no Store handle needed) | under path-as-identity the card serializes as `{path}` — no ULID to stamp, and the prior Send-safety gap dissolves |
| `mcp-tool-board-create` | done | `mcp-server/src/handler/{router,dispatch}.rs` (`board_create` → `create_board`) wraps `core::boards::ops::create_board`; returns `rel_path`+`board_id`+`status`; smoke `board_create_commits_directly_even_in_review_mode` + `board_write_tools_round_trip_direct`; toggle `mcp.tools.board_create_enabled` | DEVIATION from the generic review-mode contract: `board_create` commits directly EVEN under `review_required` — the op-log whole-file-create staging path seeds the doc by writing an empty `.md` to disk (via `OpLog::create_document`'s `write_md_file`), which would leave a phantom empty board-doc visible until the user accepted. Creates are structural; the safer fallback is direct-commit and let the user delete on reject. Subsequent board *edits* on that board still stage in review mode |
| `mcp-tool-board-add-text-card` | done | `mcp-server/src/handler/{router,dispatch}.rs` (`board_add_text_card` → `add_board_text_card`) drives `core::boards::ops::preview_edit(BoardEdit::AddTextCard)`; mints `card_id` (returned in the response); review stages via `stage_whole_body`, direct commits via `op_writes::user_save`; smoke `board_write_tools_round_trip_direct`; toggle `mcp.tools.board_add_text_card_enabled` |

Ground-truth manifest (canonical, including `.txt`) at `<out>/.synth/manifest.jsonl`. Knobs: `--txt-rate` writes a fraction as plain `.txt` (no frontmatter, per txt-ingest.md:105 leading-`---`-is-content rule); `--paste-rate` splices fixture syntax (sql/shell/json/python/tcpdump/regex) into eligible topics — fenced in `.md`, indented/raw/inline in `.txt` to exercise txt-ingest's code-region exclusion (`txt-chunker-guardrails`). Pathology mix (near-dup / topic-drift / very-short / very-long) at ~10%. Runner / scoring / recall@K still deferred until `cli-query` lands |

## Import & viewers (import.md)

External tools produce source content; hiker imports it via one manifest, displays each item through a finite built-in viewer registry, and indexes a markdown shadow. No runtime plugin system.

| Slug | Status | Notes |
| ---- | ------ | ----- |
| `import-external-producer-boundary` | planned | acquisition lives in external, vault-blind/secret-blind producers; hiker only imports the result |
| `import-manifest` | planned | tool-agnostic `manifest.json` (`{files:[{path,kind,viewer}],…}`); any producer targets it |
| `import-viewer-registry` | planned | finite built-in viewer set keyed by content kind; markdown + `hiker-htmlview` (html/warc) now, pdf/image later; adding a kind is a code change, not a plugin |
| `import-display-vs-index` | planned | original displayed via its viewer; the markdown shadow is the indexed/searched layer |
| `import-shapes` | planned | two shapes: original + `.md` sidecar shadow, or a bare `.md` note |
| `import-viewer-hint` | planned | per-item `viewer:<id>` only; no params/layout language |
| `import-warc-viewer` | planned | `.warc` viewed via the ZIM pattern (container reader + `hiker-htmlview` + offline resource provider) |
| `import-versioning-oplog` | planned | re-import = an `extractor`-authored op on accepted (op-log history/diff/restore); artifacts retained per cascade, device-local |
| `import-source-type-facet` | planned | imported notes stamp provenance so search can include/exclude by source type (pairs with `search-source-type-filter`) |
| `import-untrusted-input` | planned | importer validates manifest + paths (no traversal/escape); producer is outside the trust boundary |
| `import-core-importer` | planned | core owns the importer: place notes + archives, wikilink-rewrite cross-item links, version on re-import; shadows ingest via the normal markdown path |
| `import-raw-self-sufficient` | future | core derives its own text shadow from a raw `.html`/`.warc`/`.pdf` at rest (no sidecar); needs content-type chunking + the source-type facet first |
| `import-pdf-as-txt` | future | PDF text indexed like `.txt` (cheap deterministic chunker, `txt-ingest.md` pattern); the PDF opens in its viewer |
| `import-viewer-types-future` | future | pdf/image/audio viewers as built-in registry additions |

Offline `.zim` viewer; renders articles via `hiker-htmlview` (the `htmlview-render` renderer), subresources served offline against the `zim://` base, no JS. Archive read by `zxr`; `core` uninvolved |

## Activity registry (activity-registry.md)

The generic activity-registry family in `egui_workbench` (the shell) that app-shell consumers (sidebar mode switcher, activity bar, hamburger menu, command palette, keybind registry) iterate to discover what to render. One registry, in the shell; `hiker-app` binds it as `Activity<dyn AppCtx>`; no second concrete copy. Lifts per-activity state out of `PanelStates` and gives every feature one `app/src/features/<x>/` home. Gated behind the substrate refactor landing first; shell-side work (genericize the registry, collapse dead crates) can land independently.

| Slug | Status | Notes |
| ---- | ------ | ----- |
| `feature-trait` | done | The generic `Activity<C>` / `View<C>` (+ sub-traits) in `egui_workbench::activity` is the live family; hiker-app binds `Activity<dyn AppCtx>` for all 12 builtins. The 648-LOC concrete `Activity`/`View` twin in `app/src/activity/mod.rs` is deleted |
| `feature-registry` | done | `ActivityRegistry<dyn AppCtx>` (the shell's `ActivityRegistry<C>`: `build`/`iter`/`by_id`/`invoke`) is built at `app/src/bootstrap.rs` and stashed on `AppState::activities`; the app's concrete twin is gone |
| `feature-ctx` | done | `Ctx` is a marker base in `egui_workbench` (no methods); the app's per-surface slice rides `SurfaceCtx<'a>` (`app/src/activity/mod.rs`) carrying `services`/`toasts`/`config`/`vault`/`state: &mut dyn Any`/`active_path`/`effects`. The old `with_ctx` is now `AppCtx::surface_ctx(state_key) -> Option<SurfaceCtx<'_>>` |
| `feature-app-ctx` | done | `AppCtx: Ctx` (`app/src/activity/mod.rs`) implemented on `AppState` with a single object-safe `surface_ctx(state_key) -> Option<SurfaceCtx<'_>>` accessor (`SurfaceCtx<'a>` is the disjoint-`AppState`-field borrow). App-owned activities bind `Activity<dyn AppCtx>` and open render with `let ctx = &mut ctx.surface_ctx(self.state_key())`; shared activities bind their own minimal requirement trait (e.g. `FileTreeCtx`) that `AppCtx` supertraits. `defer` lives on `SurfaceCtx`; the `Effect` queue is `AppState::pending_effects`, drained at each surface-invocation site (debug-assert guards against cross-frame leaks) |
| `feature-feature-home` | planned | Two-tier placement rule: a feature's engine is a crate iff reusable without hiker (editor, hiker-canvas, zxr, hiker-render, hiker-llm, the shell); otherwise the feature is one directory `app/src/features/<x>/` holding its `Activity<dyn AppCtx>` impl + views + state + requirement-trait impl. Collapses the `app/src/*` + `panels/*` + `*_activity/` scatter |
| `feature-state-ownership` | partial | `app/src/state.rs` (`clusters_state`/`trails_state` top-level + `PanelStates`), `clusters/state.rs`, `trails/state.rs`. Clusters/trails/vault state lifted onto `AppState`; everything else stays in `PanelStates` (by design during migration). Target home is `app/src/features/<x>/` per `feature-feature-home`; surfaces reach state through `&mut dyn AppCtx`, not `&mut AppState` |

Trait defined; the only impl (`ClustersSidebar`) is a stub that downcasts then prints `(routed via panels_registry in v1)` — the live body still renders through `panels_registry`, not the trait |

Sidebar mode switcher resolves icon/label from the registry for migrated ids; the body still renders via `panels_registry` (the `SidebarSurface` impls are stubs, per `feature-surface-sidebar`) |

| `feature-chat-migration` | planned | Phase 3: `app/src/chat/` becomes a feature with both a sidebar surface (the docked chat region today on the right) and a panel surface (the agent tab). User can then place chat anywhere via the registry, not just the right-docked region. The legacy `chat_dock: ChatDockState` field on `PanelStates` deletes as part of this slug |
| `feature-multi-region-sidebar` | partial | `egui-workbench/src/side_panel_stack.rs` — VSCode-style accordion. `SidePanelStack<Mode>` holds an ordered `sections: Vec<Mode>` + per-section `collapsed`/height `weights` + `focused`. Activity-bar click *switches* (focus if already open keeping the arrangement, else open in full — replace the whole stack with that one section; never adds to a split); clicking the focused icon hides the bar (`workspace.rs` click handler). Dragging an activity icon out of the strip and releasing it over the window *adds* that mode as a section (VSCode "drag a view into the sidebar"): `activity_bar.rs` reports `ActivityBarResponse::dropped_out` when a drag releases outside the strip rect, publishes a `drag_active_id()` flag mid-drag, and `side_panel_stack::paint_drop_target` lights up the bar while a drag hovers it. `Render` paints each section: a uniform header (twistie + title = drag handle for reorder + host action buttons; no per-section focus highlight, no `+`/`…` buttons) and, when expanded, the host `side_bar_ui` body. Right-clicking a header opens the panel menu: host `side_bar_actions_menu` + "Add panel" submenu (unopened modes) + "Close panel". Inter-section resize handles transfer height `weights` (clamped to `MIN_BODY`, guarded against the short-window `clamp` inversion). `Workbench::open_primary_panel` (switch) / `add_primary_panel` (add section) / `set_primary_panels` (restore) + `SidePanelStack::restore` / `open_modes` / `collapsed_modes` / `section_weight` / `focused` accessors. No egui_tiles, no tab strip — a lone section looks like the old single side bar. **Persistence:** `app/src/side_panel_persist.rs` writes the arrangement (open sections + collapsed + weights + focused + visibility) to `<vault>/.hiker/side-panel.json` (own `version`, currently 1) — captured + value-compared each autosave tick (no dirty flag since the accordion mutates inside egui_workbench), restored in `bootstrap::open_vault` after tab-state restore. Tests: `egui-workbench/tests/resize.rs` + `side_panel_stack::tests` (switch / split-restore). Drag-reorder + resize need a manual smoke check |
| `feature-panel-single-accordion` | done | `app/src/panels/{related,backlinks,search}.rs` | Feature panel bodies render their content directly under the workbench accordion's section header — no inner `collapsible_header`. The Related/Backlinks/Search panels no longer wrap their results in a second collapsible (the old `discovery_pane::collapsible_header` + its module are removed). The `search.sections.*_expanded` settings are now inert |
| `feature-trash-panel` | done | `app/src/sidebar/trash.rs` (`TrashView`), `panels_registry.rs` (`PANEL_TRASH` / `P_TRASH`), `workbench_host.rs` (`HikerMode::Trash` + label/panel_id/icon/`all`), `state.rs` (`ConfirmIntent::EmptyTrash`) | Trash is a standalone activity-bar panel (folder/trash icon), not pinned inside Files. Body lists trashed items (name + deleted-at + Restore / Purge) read from `hiker_core::trash::Trash`; empty shows "Trash is empty". The batch "Empty trash" verb lives in the header right-click menu (`Host::side_bar_actions_menu` for `Trash`, disabled when empty) and routes through the `EmptyTrash` confirm modal. Removed from the Files panel (`sidebar/files.rs` no longer has `trash_bin`/`TrashTimeFmt`) |
| `feature-workbench-home` | done | `egui_workbench::activity` is *the* registry home: the generic `Activity<C>`/`View<C>`/`ActivityRegistry<C>` family + `Ctx` marker base + `ActionError` are the live registry, bound by hiker-app as `Activity<dyn AppCtx>`. The 648-LOC concrete twin in `app/src/activity/` is gone. Binding `C = dyn AppCtx` (a `'static` trait object) lets the registry live on `AppState`; the per-surface borrow comes from `AppCtx::surface_ctx() -> Option<SurfaceCtx<'_>>`, not from a borrowed type parameter — which is what makes the generic family compile without `unsafe` |
| `feature-shell-battery` | planned | Generic PKM-free activities ship in `egui_workbench` itself, feature-gated, so every consumer gets them once: filetree (`feature-filetree-shared`), find/replace, hex view (`feature-hexview-shared`). A consumer turns off the ones it doesn't want |
| `feature-shell-battery-wasm` | planned | A shell battery depends on egui + capability traits only — zero native crates in its body. Filesystem is a `Vfs` capability trait the host fills (`FileTreeCtx::vfs`); native apps supply a `std::fs` impl, wasm a in-memory/IndexedDB one. Keeps `egui_workbench` wasm-compilable; `std::fs`/`walkdir`/`rfd` live in each app's `Ctx` impl |
| `feature-filetree-shared` | planned | The filetree is a **shell battery** in `egui_workbench` (`FileTreeCtx` = `vfs` + `decorate_row` hook + `move_path`), capability-trait/VFS-pure per `feature-shell-battery-wasm`. Model on hiker-lite's pure `panels/filetree.rs` (144 LOC). hiker-app's Vault feature composes it (`feature-vault-as-feature`) |
| `feature-hexview-shared` | planned | Hex view is a **shell battery** in `egui_workbench` (stateless `show(ui, &HexBuffer)` + a tiny `HexViewCtx`); promote from `hiker-lite/src/panels/hex_view.rs`. Simplest battery — no coupling. No hex view in hiker-app today |
| `feature-crawler-shell-consumer` | planned | `hiker-crawler` lives in its own repository and is the validating second consumer: a second composition root over the `egui_workbench` submodule + shared crates (`hiker-llm`, `hiker-theme`) + the shell batteries it reuses (editor/buffer, filetree, find/replace, command palette, chat). A shared seam earns its keep iff the crawler pulls through it cleanly; PKM-only features (clusters/trails/boards/graph/embeddings) stay app-only and are never touched for it |
| `feature-hiker-lite-adoption` | planned | hiker-lite is a minimal "no PKM" consumer/editor demo on the shell (file search / find-replace / hex view as shell batteries) — a convenient smoke build, not the driver of the design (that's `feature-crawler-shell-consumer`) |
| `feature-vault-as-feature` | planned | The Vault feature (`app/src/features/vault/`) *composes* the shell filetree battery: supplies a `std::fs` `Vfs` and layers hiker decorations via `FileTreeCtx::decorate_row` (index markers, trail/board rows + menus) + the referrer-rewriting `move_path`. Lands as an `Activity<dyn AppCtx>` from day one — no separate migration. Coupled to `feature-filetree-shared` |


## Inbox rules (inbox-rules.md)

Deterministic, user-authored auto-org for newly-created notes. Sibling to the AI-driven Trees system. All planned.

| Slug | Status | Notes |
| ---- | ------ | ----- |
| `inbox-rules` | partial | first-match-wins rule list in `[inbox].rules` (per `settings-section-inbox`); each rule matches basename regex and/or first-4KB body regex, action is `move_to` (folder) and/or `add_tag` (frontmatter tag). Engine in `core/src/inbox.rs` (`Rules::compile` / `apply_to_created`), fired from the **indexer's** Created handler (`core/src/indexer/jobs.rs::run_inbox_rules`) for indexable extensions; routes through `move-note-core-cmd` + the frontmatter-merge primitive. Toast with Undo. Unaffected by `llm-features-disable-entirely`. **Partial: no UI** — rules are config-only (raw `[inbox].rules` TOML); no settings editor / rule-management surface yet |


## Vault view (vault-view.md)

A second sidebar mode presenting the vault as a read-only logical lens over the on-disk Files tree — the follow-on that makes crawl-job nesting and source grouping legible. All planned; lands after the crawl pipeline.

| Slug | Status | Notes |
| ---- | ------ | ----- |

Sidebar mode reachable from the activity bar; `⋯` hosts the lens picker. **Gap:** mode choice not yet persisted to `vault.sidebar_mode` (resets to Files on relaunch) |
| `vault-view-readonly-lens` | done | `app/src/vault_view/mod.rs` renders a read-only tree — click opens (preview-slot / Mod-click sticky), no drag-to-reorganize, no placement state stored. Lenses: `Composed` (default, the metadata-derived groupings below) + by-folder (flattened) + flat, picked from `⋯` |
| `vault-view-crawl-nesting` | done | `app/src/vault_view/tree.rs:build_crawl_nodes` nests every note whose `hiker.parent` stamp resolves to a capture/manifest note's id under that note (`NodeKind::Capture`); a folder-mate with **no** stamp, or a dangling stamp, stays a normal node (tested: `crawl_children_nest_by_parent_stamp`, `dangling_parent_stamp_is_not_nested`). Stamp authority, not folder membership; data read once via `Store::notes_with_meta` |
| `vault-view-trail-nesting` | done | `app/src/vault_view/tree.rs:build_trail_nodes`+`nest_waypoints` nests a trail-doc's waypoints by the resolved `trail_waypoints` tree (`parent_waypoint_id` + `tree_path`, re-derived from `hiker.waypoints` on ingest), preserving order + side-trail branching (tested: `trail_waypoints_nest_with_side_trail_order`). Whole table read once via `Store::all_trail_waypoints` |
| `vault-view-sidecar-surfacing` | done | `app/src/vault_view/tree.rs:build_sidecar_nodes` detects `<src>.<ext>.md` sidecars (`sidecar_source`) and renders the non-md source as a synthetic node with the sidecar nested (tested: `sidecar_source_detection`, `sidecar_pairs_with_synthetic_source`). **GAP:** synthetic source node opens the source path via `editor_pane::open_file` rather than a Files-mode reveal verb — fine for indexed sidecars, "view original" verb is followup |
| `vault-view-source-groups` | done | `app/src/vault_view/tree.rs:build_source_groups` groups leftover notes by the authorship trichotomy (`hiker.author`/`hiker.provenance` via `author_bucket`); chat sessions collapse into a "Sessions" bucket with an "Imported" sub-bucket, labelled by date+id (`session_label`) not filename (tested: `source_groups_split_by_authorship_and_sessions`). **NOT blocked on a new index column** — the existing `note_meta` index (v8, `store-note-metadata-index`) already holds `hiker.author`/`provenance`/`parent`/`kind`; the lens reads them in one query (`core/src/store/metadata.rs:notes_with_meta`), no per-note disk reads, no schema bump |
| `vault-view-row-previews` | done | `app/src/vault_view/mod.rs:render_node`+`row_tree_thumbnail` renders the reusable force-directed preview thumbnail (`widgets::preview::thumbnail`) before the label of any `hiker.kind: cluster-tree` row; tree id is the note's filename stem, nodes loaded read-only via `trees.list_nodes`. Canvas thumbnails wire into the canvases activity (`.canvas` files aren't indexed, so they don't surface in the Vault lens). Full preview surface in `previews.md` |
| `vault-view-membership-groups` | planned | deferred — trail / cluster membership as additional Vault-mode groupings |
| `vault-view-saved-lenses` | planned | deferred — user-defined grouping rules (tag facet / folder glob / frontmatter field) saved and re-selectable |


## Previews (previews.md)

Reusable rich-row preview thumbnails: a tiny inline render of a `.canvas` shape or a cluster-tree graph next to a list row, hover-expandable into a non-occluding floating preview, cached under `.hiker/previews/`. A `ThumbnailProvider` trait is the domain seam; the widget owns rendering, the cache, and the hover-expand.

| Slug | Status | Evidence | Notes |
| ---- | ------ | -------- | ----- |
| `preview-thumbnail-provider` | done | `app/src/widgets/preview/` (`mod.rs`: `ThumbnailProvider`, `PreviewKey`, `PreviewKind`, `PREVIEW_RENDER_VERSION`, `content_hash`; `thumbnail.rs`: the `thumbnail(ui, provider, vault_root, opts)` widget) | Domain-agnostic: provider computes a stable key + rasterizes pixels; the widget owns the cache↔texture round-trip + hover registration. Render version folded into every key so a renderer change invalidates all kinds (tested) |
| `preview-canvas-thumbnail` | done | `app/src/panels/canvas/thumbnail.rs:CanvasPreview` (`render` + `expanded_paint`), `app/src/widgets/preview/mod.rs` (`ExpandedPaint` hook + `paint_live_expanded_area`), `app/src/widgets/preview/thumbnail.rs` (stashes the thunk) | TWO paths, one per size. TINY inline thumbnail: a cached SVG now drawn LOD-FAITHFUL to match the live renderer at fit zoom (`canvas-lod-placeholder`) — translucent group rects + header label band, curved edge connectors with arrowheads, rounded cards with a title line + 2–3 decreasing skeleton bars in the node color; rasterized via `render::rasterize_svg`, content hash over the bytes (tested: card + curved-edge + group-band SVG). EXPANDED hover preview: LIVE-PAINTS the real renderer via `canvas_view::CanvasView::show_static` (`canvas-static-paint`) zoom-to-fit inside the non-interactable `Area`, no capture and NO `.hiker` cache entry (live every frame); parsed `Canvas` memoized per content-hash on a UI-thread-local (bounded) so a hover doesn't re-parse each frame; parse failure → `None` → falls back to nothing. `PREVIEW_RENDER_VERSION` bumped (1→2) to retire old sketches |
| `preview-tree-thumbnail` | done | `app/src/panels/cluster_thumbnail.rs:TreeThumbnail` | Loads a tree's nodes, runs a DETERMINISTIC seeded fixed-iteration `hiker_graph::force_layout` (convergence early-out off) — not the live async `LayoutWorker` — so the cache key is reproducible; fits + emits dots(nodes)+lines(edges) SVG. Node count capped. Shape hash ignores summary/policy, tracks re-parent (tested: determinism, cap, hash) |
| `preview-hover-expand-side-anchor` | done | `app/src/widgets/preview/mod.rs:render_expanded_preview` (called once post-sidebar from `main.rs::update`) | Stashes `(key, anchor_rect, hover_start, live_paint?)` in egui memory on hover; draws the large preview in a non-interactable `Order::Tooltip` `Area` so it never senses the pointer → never steals row hover. ~120ms debounce, side-anchored (right→flip-left→clamp via the shared `expanded_area_min` helper), drops stale requests so it vanishes on pointer-off. Two render branches behind one placement: a provider that opted into `expanded_paint` (canvas) live-paints its thunk into the rect (clipped); otherwise the cached large PNG is blitted (trees) — `show_static` registers no interactive widget, so the live branch is held to the same no-pointer-sense rule |
| `preview-note-hover` | done | `app/src/widgets/preview/note.rs` (`register_note_hover` + `render_note_preview`, called from `main.rs::update` beside `render_expanded_preview`); `app/src/widgets/preview/mod.rs` (`expanded_area_min` + `EXPAND_HOLD_SECS` made `pub(crate)`); wired in `app/src/backlinks/mod.rs`, `app/src/related/mod.rs` (the related view now renders plain icon+title rows with the hover-preview, replacing the rich `result_card`s — `result_card` stays for the search panel), `app/src/appears_in/mod.rs::draw_ref_row` | Hovering a context-panel note row shows a read-only **markdown-with-diagrams** preview of the note in the same non-interactable `Order::Tooltip` `Area` (hold + stale-drop + side-anchor reused from `preview-hover-expand-side-anchor`). Renders via `buffer_view::show_embedded_buffer` (`EmbedOpts { read_only:true, markdown:true, focus:false }`) — needs `&mut AppState` (diagram cache / shared buffer / title resolver, all `!Send`), so it runs at the frame loop, NOT through the `Send+Sync` `ExpandedPaint` thunk; a parallel egui-memory slot (`preview-note-hover-request`), not the thumbnail `HoverRequest`. Per-preview `EmbeddedView` in a UI-thread-local (recreated on path change) so `&mut embed` never aliases `&mut app` — the `canvas-inline-edit` pattern. No disk cache (live render). `appears-in` `.canvas` rows show a hover-only `CanvasPreview` spatial preview (plain canvas icon in the row, preview on hover via `register_hover_only` — no inline thumbnail) |
| `preview-toggle` | done | `core/src/config/mod.rs` (`Ui::hover_previews_enabled`, default true) + `patch.rs` (eligible bool, both scopes); gate in `app/src/main.rs::update` (skips `render_expanded_preview` + `render_note_preview` when off); `app/src/workbench_host.rs::hover_preview_eye` + the `side_bar_action_buttons` eye, shown for `context/backlinks` / `context/appears-in` / `context/related` / `vault` / `canvases` | An eye button in every preview-showing sidebar view's section header opens a **Show hover previews** toggle (`[ui].hover_previews_enabled`, `Scope::Vault`). Off suppresses the hover-expand popups (canvas / tree / note) — registration still runs, only the draw is gated; always-on inline thumbnails (vault cluster-tree rows, canvases activity) stay. One global flag, surfaced per-view for discoverability |
| `cluster-tree-open-routing` | done | `app/src/editor_pane.rs::open_file` (branch after `.canvas`); `core/src/trees/store.rs::Db::tree_id_at_path` (reads `hiker.kind`/`hiker.id` frontmatter) | A cluster-tree note (`hiker.kind: cluster-tree`) opens in its force-graph `ClusterGraph` tab — `find_or_open_tab` keyed on the frontmatter `hiker.id` — not as raw markdown; it has no in-buffer view (unlike a board's Markdown toggle, so a buffer would just show YAML). Central in `open_file`, so it fires from every caller: vault sidebar, backlinks, related, appears-in, wikilinks, Back/Forward. Boards keep their existing file-tree `is_board_doc` routing |
| `preview-disk-cache` | done | `app/src/widgets/preview/cache.rs:PreviewCache` | PNG at `<vault>/.hiker/previews/<kind>-<hash:016x>-<size>.png`; small/large are distinct `size` buckets; hit→texture, miss→render+write. Best-effort byte-budget LRU sweep (64 MB, oldest-by-mtime, once/session) mirroring the diagram disk-cache. All I/O degrades to live render (tested: round-trip, buckets, kind-collision, sweep) |


## Wikilinks (wikilinks.md)

Path-based note references authored and stored as names. Stored form is `[[Name]]` (unique basename) or `[[folder/Name]]` (disambiguated). Existing ULID-form implementation needs rework: parsing, decoration, autocomplete, normalize-on-save, resolution, backlinks, and unresolved-handling all need to move from ULID-keyed to path-keyed. Rename now rewrites every referrer (wikilink bodies + trail/kanban paths) through a single shared pass.

| Slug | Status | Notes |
| ---- | ------ | ----- |
| `wikilink-path-form` | done | `core/src/wikilink.rs` (`parse_links`, `ParsedLink`) | parser drops ULID detection + `\|display` alias half; the body is the target verbatim (bare basename or `folder/sub/Name`). The save-time normalize pass was previously removed |
| `wikilink-render` | partial | `editor/editor-md/src/links.rs` (`wikilink_decorations()`); `app/src/panels/buffer/wikilink_nav.rs` (`title_resolver`) | decoration parses path-form bodies and renders the resolver-provided title (basename today); frontmatter `title` lookup deferred until the indexer surface for that lands |
| `wikilink-autocomplete` | done | `app/src/completion_sources.rs` (`WikilinkSource`) | inserts `wikilink::shortest_unambiguous(paths, rel)` — bare basename when vault-unique, minimal folder-prefix when not |
| `wikilink-hand-typed` | done | `core/src/wikilink.rs` (`resolve_path`) | bare-name + explicit-path resolution via `resolve_path`; no rewrite on save, no stamping. The click resolver in `wikilink_nav.rs` consumes it directly |
| `wikilink-ambiguous-resolution` | done | `core/src/wikilink.rs` (`AmbiguityPolicy`, `resolve_path`); `core/src/config/sections.rs` (`WikilinksConfig`, `AmbiguousResolution`) | vault-scope `[wikilinks] ambiguous_resolution = "unresolved" \| "lex-first" \| "nearest-folder"`, default `"unresolved"`. Lex-first sorts and picks first; nearest-folder counts shared path-segment prefix with the referrer |
| `wikilink-case-sensitivity` | planned | path equality follows host FS rules; cross-platform vaults inherit case-sensitive; sync layer warns on case-only collisions |
| `wikilink-rename-rewrite` | done | `core/src/links_rename.rs` (`on_note_moved` → trails + boards + wikilink-body sweep, `rewrite_wikilink_bodies` + `splice_link_bodies`); `core/src/indexer/jobs.rs` (single call site replaces the prior `run_trails_on_note_moved` / `run_boards_on_note_moved` pair on `IndexJob::Rename` / `Move` / `MoveFolder`); `core/src/trails/ops.rs` (`write_with_suppress_and_reindex_for_links` re-export) | shared rename-rewrite pass over all three referrer types runs as one orchestrator from the indexer; each domain is best-effort (errors logged, never propagated). Wikilink bodies use `wikilink::shortest_unambiguous` for the replacement form (same picker rule autocomplete uses). The Bloom-filter optimization is intentionally skipped (`wikilink-rename-bloom-filter-deferred`) — the straightforward `walk_indexable_files` enumerator lands first |
| `wikilink-rename-bloom-filter-deferred` | planned | deferred optimization: Bloom over "note contains any wikilinks" to skip the no-link majority on rename; build the straightforward enumerator first |
| `wikilink-resolve` | done | `core/src/wikilink.rs` (`resolve_path`) | bare-name matches every path with the matching basename; explicit-path matches the exact vault path; ambiguity routed through `AmbiguityPolicy` (config-driven). No `path_for_id` / `id_for_path` indirection. Backlinks scan + click flow consume this resolver directly |
| `wikilink-click-open` | partial | `app/src/panels/buffer/mod.rs` (`open_wikilink_at`) + `app/src/panels/buffer/wikilink_nav.rs` (`open_at`) | path-form click resolves through `wikilink::resolve_path` with the vault's `[wikilinks] ambiguous_resolution` policy and the referrer's path. The `WidgetClick` plumbing in `panels/buffer/mod.rs` itself is owned by the user's in-flight click-pattern refactor — left untouched |
| `wikilink-backlinks` | partial | `app/src/panels/backlinks.rs` (`scan_backlinks`) | scan now resolves every parsed link via `wikilink::resolve_path` and matches against the active path. Still O(total bytes) per scan — the spec'd reverse-index (`structural index records source-path → target-path edges`) lands with `wikilink-rename-rewrite` |
| `wikilink-unresolved` | partial | `editor/editor-md/src/links.rs` (`COLOR_WIKILINK_UNRESOLVED`); `app/src/panels/buffer/wikilink_nav.rs` (`open_at` → `create_and_open`) | unresolved style + create-on-click stays; ambiguous-under-unresolved currently surfaces as a toast ("Multiple notes named …") — the disambiguation picker UI is the remaining gap |
| `wikilink-headings-blocks` | planned | deferred — `[[Name#Heading]]` / `[[Name#^block]]` (block needs an auto-injected anchor in the target) |
| `wikilink-embed` | planned | deferred — `![[Name]]` transclusion rendering the target's content/image/PDF inline |
| `wikilink-hover-preview` | done | `app/src/panels/buffer/wikilink_nav.rs` (`HoverState`, `track_hover`, `resolve_preview`) + `app/src/panels/graph.rs` (`paint_preview_card_with` scrollable variant) | ~400ms hover on a resolved pill shows a scrollable preview card painted on a tooltip-layer over the editor. Title is frontmatter `title` if present, else basename; body is the first ~30 lines after `skip_frontmatter`. Pointer-leave dismisses after a 200ms grace (covers pill→card slide). Scroll wheel over the card scrolls only the card body. Unresolved / ambiguous pills get no card. Mod-click closes the card and opens the target sticky via `wikilink-click-open`. Reuses `paint_preview_card` styling shared with `cluster-editor-graph-view-hover-preview-card` |
| `url-classify` | partial | `core/src/url.rs` (`classify`, `LinkTarget`, `resolve_path`) | pure, store-free, UI-free link-shape classifier — the single source of truth for "what kind of target is this link string?". Distinguishes `External` (`http(s)`/`mailto:`), `Zim { archive, article }` (mirrors `zim.rs`'s `zim://<authority>/<rest>` shape), `VaultPath` (slash- or known-extension-shaped), and `Wikilink` (`[[…]]` or bare name, alias/`#section` stripped). Precedence: brackets → external scheme → `zim://` → path shape → bare name. Optional `resolve_path` helper takes the existence/name lookups as closures so the module stays store-free. `app/src/panels/zim.rs::resolve_href` routes its external-scheme bail-out through `classify`; the interactive-diagram click path (`widget-mermaid-links`) consumes both `classify` and `resolve_path` via `diagram_nav::dispatch_link`. Remaining: route the wikilink pill click path through it too (still on `wikilink::resolve_path` directly) |

Four wiring points: startup restore copies `ts.canvas_views` into the session map; apply-on-create restores once per pane and suppresses fresh-create `fit_pending`; capture-on-close snapshots before the pane is dropped; capture-on-persist snapshots every open canvas tab into the snapshot. "Add to canvas" (`add_file_node`) routes through `working` when open / `user_save` when closed |

Hovered resize square and connector circle grow `HOVER_GROW` = 1.3x about their center; the hovered group's header band brightens (`gamma_multiply` 0.22→0.4). Purely visual, no state change. `grown_about_center` unit-tested (grows + stays centered, identity at 1.0) |
| `canvas-lod-placeholder` | done | `hiker-canvas/view/src/paint.rs` (`is_tiny`/`lod_title`/`paint_lod_placeholder`, `node_card` branch), `hiker-canvas/view/src/widget.rs` (`handle_zoom` wheel routing) | below ~150px on-screen a card skips the content engine and paints a cheap title + skeleton placeholder; collapsed zoom-to-fit on 121 nodes from 81ms to 0.04ms/frame (profiled via `tools/profile-canvas`). A wheel over a placeholder (no scrollable content) passes through to camera zoom via the shared `is_tiny` check, so a tiny card crossing the cursor mid-zoom-out doesn't stall the zoom |
| `canvas-static-paint` | done | `hiker-canvas/view/src/widget.rs` (`CanvasView::show_static`, shares the private `paint_scene` helper with `show`), `hiker-canvas/view/src/content.rs` (`NoContentRenderer`) | A paint-only display render: `show_static(ui, &Canvas, &mut dyn NodeContentRenderer)` paints the SCENE only (grid + group backgrounds + edges + cards / LOD placeholders) at the current camera with NO interaction — no `ui.interact` surface, no input (zoom/keys/pointer), no overlays/handles/context menu, nothing committed. It calls the same `paint_scene` the interactive `show` does (one helper, no duplication, keeps `show` under the length budget). Safe inside a non-interactable `Area`: registers no interactive widget, so it can't steal row hover. Zero-cost `NoContentRenderer` (paints nothing, echoes card scroll) lets a caller skip the `!Send` per-node content engine — at fit/thumbnail zoom every node is a LOD placeholder, so the renderer is never invoked anyway. First consumer: the canvas expanded hover preview (`preview-canvas-thumbnail`). Tested via `egui_kittest` (drives one frame, asserts the ctx wants no pointer/keyboard input) + a `NoContentRenderer` scroll-echo test |
| `canvas-inline-edit` | done | `app/src/panels/canvas/edit.rs` (overlay + `EDIT_VIEWS` thread-local + `forget`/`enter`/`is_editable`/`show_overlay`); `render.rs::activate_or_edit` / `resolve_edit_overlay` / `render_edit_overlay` / `persist_text_edit`; `Pane::editing` in `mod.rs`; `widget.rs::node_screen_rect` / `is_node_lod`; `buffer_view.rs` edit-preview popup + `EmbedOpts::focus`; `edit::forget` in `editor_pane::close_tab` | cards editable in place: double-click a full-detail File/Text card → foreground `Area` overlay over the node rect (captures kbd+pointer, tracks rect via `node_screen_rect` on pan/zoom; Esc / click-out / select-other / off-screen exit). Overlay seeds at the card's current scroll (`card_scroll`), no jump to top. Entry gestures (2026-06-03): double-click, **click-again on the already-sole-selected node** (widget `CanvasResponse.edit_requested`), and **Enter/F2 on a single selected editable node** (app-side, focus-gated), all through one `try_enter_edit` seam; a bright accent outline marks the active overlay. Save routing (`handle_canvas_save`, 2026-06-03): Ctrl/Cmd+S **always commits the canvas document** AND saves a single SELECTED File node's note buffer (keyed on selection via `editing_file_path`, not just inline-edit, so re-selecting after click-out still flushes); `save_canvas_document` pokes + toasts only on a real `Ok(true)` commit (a no-op no longer fakes a save). [bug-canvas-save-does-not-sync, bug-canvas-inline-edit-discoverability] File node → edits the shared note buffer via `embedded-buffer-view` (typing shows in any tab, one dirty buffer); Text node → transient editor reconciled to its own `text` via `canvas-edit-ops` `SetText` through the `persist_canvas` op-log path. Per-edit `EmbeddedView` / transient `Editor` parked in a `TabId`-keyed thread-local (off `AppState`) so `show_embedded_buffer` holds `&mut app` + `&mut embed` without aliasing — mirrors `content::PANES`. Read path unchanged (cards read live `working` buffer; LOD placeholder double-click opens a tab via the existing `activate_node`). Diagram preview-while-typing added to the `embedded-buffer-view` primitive (disjoint `buffers[path]` immut / `panels.edit_preview` mut), so every embed gets it; the buffer tab doesn't use the primitive, so no double-fire. Context-follows-edit: while a File node is in inline-edit, `canvas::inline_edited_note(app, tab_id)` (reads `Pane::editing` + the node's `file`) surfaces the edited note as the activity `Ctx::active_path` (override in `activity::with_ctx`, ahead of the canvas tab's own `.canvas` path), so the context panel (backlinks / related / appears-in) tracks the note you're editing on the canvas rather than the `.canvas` file. Verified: `cargo test -p hiker-app -p canvas-view` 237 pass; clippy clean in touched files (`-D` budget lints); `profile-canvas` zoom-to-fit 0.06ms p50 |

## Projection (projection.md)

| Slug | Status | Evidence | Notes |
| ---- | ------ | -------- | ----- |
| `proj-seam` | planned | — | generalize the graph `screen_mapper` (`force_graph.rs`) + canvas `Camera` (`camera.rs`) into a swappable `Projection` over `world_to_screen`/`screen_to_world`; affine is one impl; pure view state |
| `proj-euclidean-storage` | planned | — | hyperbolic is a lens over Euclidean coords; no `{p,q}` tiling / anchor-identity storage model (rejects the reference app's approach) |
| `proj-geometry` | planned | — | egui-free complex / Möbius / geodesic-sampling math (~200 lines, ported from reference) in a dedicated `hiker-projection` crate beside `hiker-graph` |
| `proj-magnification` | planned | — | projection reports a local scale factor (`(1−|z|²)` Poincaré / fisheye derivative) driving node+card size, label visibility, LOD |
| `proj-fisheye` | planned | — | radial focus+context lens (`r'=tanh(k·r)` or graphical fisheye) over the affine map; no mode switch; the cheap shared substrate |
| `proj-card-scale` | planned | — | canvas cards stay axis-aligned rects at projected center with per-card uniform scale (no glyph shearing) |
| `proj-poincare` | planned | — | Poincaré disk projection into the unit disk; conformal object scaling; the showpiece mode on the seam |
| `proj-poincare-mode` | planned | — | Poincaré is a navigate-only `ViewMode` toggled into/out of for editing on the canvas (Canvas/JSON-toggle pattern); a plain view option on the graph |
| `proj-mobius-pan` | planned | — | drag re-centers the disk via a Möbius transform (no scroll-off); per-frame re-centering sidesteps boundary-precision re-anchoring |
| `proj-flyto` | planned | — | animated Möbius fly-to glides a clicked/opened node to disk center (cubic ease-out); pure view state, hooks `nav-stack` |
| `proj-geodesic-edges` | planned | — | edges draw as geodesic arcs (sampled polylines) under curved projections; curvature encodes hyperbolic distance |
| `proj-boundary` | planned | — | draw the unit-disk boundary + rim alpha fade near `|z|→1` (mirrors the reference shader) |
| `proj-lod-ladder` | planned | — | magnification-coupled LOD reusing `canvas-lod-placeholder` / label-min-zoom: full → title dot → edge-marker toward the rim |
| `proj-graph-mode` | planned | — | projection modes apply to all `graph_view` consumers (vault `Graph` tab + cluster graphs); primary target (no flat-card problem) |
| `proj-canvas-mode` | planned | — | `.canvas` board adopts the seam with `proj-card-scale`; Euclidean coords + canonical-JSON op-log binding untouched |
| `proj-minimap` | planned | — | always-on corner Poincaré minimap overview via the paint-only `canvas-static-paint` path; distinct from the affine `canvas-minimap` |
| `proj-minimap-expand` | planned | — | click the minimap to promote the disk to full pane while the Euclidean view demotes into the corner (animated swap entrypoint) |
| `proj-minimap-shape` | planned | — | per-view circle ↔ square framing of the disk/minimap |
| `proj-view-toggle` | planned | — | View/eye-menu projection selector (Off/Fisheye/Poincaré) per surface; default Off restores exact affine; persisted as view state |
| `proj-config-live` | planned | — | live config sub-menu in the View menu; controls show per active mode, apply next frame, view state only |
| `proj-cfg-strength` | planned | — | slider: distortion strength `k` (distance→radius mapping / disk spread) |
| `proj-cfg-focus-mode` | planned | — | focus source: cursor-follow / locked-center / follow-selection |
| `proj-cfg-size-falloff` | planned | — | slider: how strongly node/card size tracks magnification toward the rim (0 uniform → 1 full conformal) |
| `proj-cfg-card-scale-clamp` | planned | — | min/max per-card scale clamp (canvas) so rim cards stay clickable, center cards don't explode |
| `proj-cfg-geodesic` | planned | — | geodesic edges on/off + segment count (chord vs smooth arc) |
| `proj-cfg-boundary-fade` | planned | — | boundary circle on/off + fade start radius + fade strength |
| `proj-cfg-lod-thresholds` | planned | — | magnification cutoffs for full → dot → edge-marker LOD ladder |
| `proj-cfg-flyto` | planned | — | fly-to on/off + animation duration |
| `proj-cfg-minimap` | planned | — | minimap on/off + corner + size + circle/square shape |
| `proj-h3-3d` | planned | — | deferred 3D hyperbolic (unit-ball) riding `scene3d-shared`, not this 2D seam |
| `proj-alt-models` | planned | — | deferred Beltrami–Klein / upper-half-plane projections as alternate impls on the same seam |
| `proj-multi-focus` | planned | — | deferred two-focus elliptical/bipolar lens to compare neighborhoods |


## Ideas (ideas.md)

Deferred ideas with named slugs but no spec doc of their own yet. Each row points at the `ideas.md` entry for context; a real spec doc will land if/when the feature is picked up.

| Slug | Status | Notes |
| ---- | ------ | ----- |
| `browser-extension-capture` | planned | companion browser extension with two primary buttons — "Save to Hiker" (one-click capture into `inbox/`, an external producer feeding the import path, `import.md`) and "Save to Hiker and append to active trail" (same capture, plus append as waypoint to the active trail, greyed when no active trail). Talks to the running app over the existing localhost MCP server; no new transport. Phone share-sheet / Android intent capture is the sibling future on mobile. Anchor: `ideas.md` "Capture surfaces" |
| `drawio-source-ingest` | planned | future ingestion source — `.drawio` (and `.drawio.svg` / `.drawio.png`) diagrams as a source type; structural skeleton (nodes, edges, labels) extracted into a derived hiker note for search/related-notes coverage; diagram file kept as canonical artifact. Anchor: `ideas.md` "Source ingestion" |
| `drawio-mcp-tools` | planned | future MCP tool family for drawio diagrams — `get_diagram` / `add_node` / `add_edge` / `update_node`; lets an attached agent read and incrementally edit the diagram file in place via the drawio XML format. Layout round-tripping is the hard part; v1 likely restricted to structural-only edits. Anchor: `ideas.md` "Source ingestion" |
| `cluster-chunk-thread-hint` | planned | parallel chunk-level clustering: tightly-clustered chunks across notes surface as "thread" hints (not auto-trails). Anchor: `ideas.md` "Clustering" |
| `cluster-chunk-multitopic-flag` | planned | parallel chunk-level clustering: a note whose chunks scatter across clusters is a split candidate. Anchor: `ideas.md` "Clustering" |
| `servo-warc-capture` | planned | high-fidelity `.warc` capture by an external producer (more faithful than a single-file HTML snapshot); hiker views it via the warc viewer (`import-warc-viewer`), md shadow stays the search layer. Anchor: `ideas.md` "Viewers" |
| `agent-session-partitions` | planned | enforced partitions so no agent session holds all three prompt-injection trifecta legs at once (untrusted content + vault-write + arbitrary-net); in-process generalization of the import trust boundary (`import-external-producer-boundary`). Touches `mcp.md`/chat/`task-queue.md`; own spec when picked up. Anchor: `ideas.md` "Agent session capabilities" |
